Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a organic algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 773. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 695/1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to organic theory, we decompose the problem into 773 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 695. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
